三点观察：① 一道推导（高斯似然）就占了 40 分里的 15 分，必须能流畅地默写出来。② 大部分简答题问的是"为什么"：为什么叫链接函数、为什么要参考类、为什么大多数项为 0、为什么不能最小化风险。这正是本讲义每一步「为什么」要讲的东西。③ 题目用的是课件的记号（$f_\beta$、$\phi$、$\theta_k$），答题时也用课件记号。

一、选择题 · 每题 1 分

E1选择题：答案之外，更要知道错的为什么错

1 Linear regression is linear in (   ) — A the input variable · B the coefficients · C the noise · D the output variable

答案 B。模型 $f_\beta(x) = \beta^\top\phi(x)$ 对每个系数 $\beta_i$ 都是线性的，而 $\phi(x)$ 可以是 $x^2$、$\sin x$ 这类非线性函数，所以 A 错。多项式回归就是对 $x$ 非线性、对系数线性的"线性回归"。C、D 不是"线性回归"中"线性"的含义。见 3.2、4.2。

2 Supervised learning involves (   ) — A design points and targets · B inputs and design points · C targets and outputs · D only inputs

答案 A。Lecture 2 的术语：输入叫设计点，输出叫目标值，监督学习需要成对的 $(x_n, y_n)$。B 中两者都是输入，没有输出；C 中两者都是输出，没有输入；D 是无监督学习。这道题考的是术语，要认得 design points = inputs、targets = outputs。见 3.1、1.5。

3 A model $y = \beta^Tx + \varepsilon$ with augmented input $x$, weights $\beta\in\mathbb R^m$ and $N$ data points: the design matrix is of order (   ) — A $N\times N$ · B $m\times m$ · C $N\times m$ · D $N\times(m-1)$

答案 C。设计矩阵每行一个数据点（$N$ 行），每列一个权重（$m$ 列）。陷阱在 D："augmented"说明常数 1 已经包含在 $x$ 里，所以列数就是权重个数 $m$，不用再减或加 1。A 是核矩阵 / 帽子矩阵的大小，B 是 $\Phi^\top\Phi$ 的大小。见 3.4。

4 If the model complexity is too high, there is a tendency to (   ) — A underfit · B overfit · C have a low model variance · D have a high model bias

答案 B。高复杂度 → 过拟合 → 高方差、低偏差，所以 C、D 都说反了；A 对应低复杂度。见 3.5、4.3。

5 Regularization can (   ) — A penalise high model complexity · B lead to a higher model complexity · C introduce wild oscillations in model parameter values · D lead to higher model parameter values

答案 A。惩罚项 $\lambda\Omega(\beta)$ 正是为了惩罚复杂度。B、C、D 都是正则化要消除的现象：课件 p.39 的表格里，没有正则化时系数巨大且正负剧烈振荡，正则化把它们收缩回来。见 3.6。

6 Which model is linearizable? — A $\beta_0 + \beta_1e^{\beta_2x}$ · B $\beta_0x^2 + \beta_1e^{\beta_2x}$ · C $\beta_0e^{\beta_1x^2 + \beta_2x}$ · D $\beta_0 + \beta_1e^{\beta_2x^2 + \beta_3x}$

答案 C。取对数：$\log y = \log\beta_0 + \beta_1x^2 + \beta_2x$，对参数 $(\log\beta_0, \beta_1, \beta_2)$ 线性，基函数是 $(1, x^2, x)$。

判断规则：乘积、幂、指数可以靠取对数拆开；一旦有相加的项，就拆不开了（$\log(a + b)$ 无法化简）。A、D 前面都多了一个加上去的 $\beta_0$，B 是两项之和，所以都不能线性化。见 4.5。

7 A hold-out set when constructing a neural network (   ) — A is part of the training set · B is used for validation during training · C is used for assessing the generalization of a model · D is a corrupt set that is discarded

答案 C。留出集（测试集）在训练和调参时都不碰，最后用它估计泛化误差。B 描述的是验证集（训练过程中用来调超参数或早停）。验证集一旦参与了选择模型，就不能再无偏地估计泛化误差，所以要另外留一份。A、D 显然不对。见 3.5、3.7。

8 In a feedforward neural network (   ) — A the hidden layer provides the outputs · B the output layer is a linear combination of inputs · C the activations in the hidden layer sum to 1 · D none of statements A, B and C are true

答案 D。A 错：输出由输出层给出，隐藏层产生的是特征。B 错：输出层是最后一个隐藏层激活的线性组合 $h = Vz_L + c$，不是输入的线性组合（否则整个网络就是线性的）。C 错：加起来等于 1 的是 softmax 输出层的概率，不是隐藏层激活。见 4.10。

9 A kernel function is (   ) — A a feature map · B the dot product of points in an input space · C the dot product of points in a feature space · D the dot product between a point in input space and a point in feature space

答案 C。$k(x,x') = \phi(x)^\top\phi(x')$ 是两个点在特征空间里的内积（只是不用显式算出 $\phi$）。A 说的是 $\phi$ 本身；B 只是线性核这个特例；D 中两个点在不同空间，没有意义。见 4.7。

10 Kernel substitution (   ) — A involves replacing a linear kernel with a general kernel · B involves replacing a general kernel with a linear kernel · C turns a nonlinear method into a linear method · D does none of A, B or C

答案 A。在对偶形式里，把线性核 $\phi(x)^\top\phi(x')$（显式特征）换成一般的核 $k(x,x')$。B 方向反了；C 也反了：核替换是把线性方法变成非线性方法。见 4.6、4.7。

二、简答与计算 · 40 分

E2简答题：底层逻辑 + 满分答案模板

1 Linear regression $y = f_\beta(x) + \varepsilon$ with $f_\beta(x) = \beta^T\phi(x)$ and Gaussian noise $\varepsilon\sim\mathcal N(0,\sigma^2)$; data $y = (y_1,\dots,y_N)^T$ with inputs $X = \{x_1,\dots,x_N\}$. (a) Derive the distribution for a single output $p(y\mid x)$. (5 marks) (b) Derive the distribution (likelihood) $p(y\mid X)$. (10 marks)

官方答案：(a) $y\mid x\sim\mathcal N(f_\beta(x), \sigma^2)$，理由是给定参数后 $f_\beta(x)$ 不是随机的，随机性只来自噪声，一个非随机量加上正态变量仍是正态，只是均值平移。(b) 假设数据（在给定参数时）独立抽取、误差 i.i.d.，则 $p_\theta(y\mid X) = \prod_n\mathcal N(y_n\mid f_\beta(x_n),\sigma^2) = \big(\frac{1}{2\pi\sigma^2}\big)^{N/2}\exp\big(-\frac{1}{2\sigma^2}\sum_n(y_n - \beta^\top\phi(x_n))^2\big)$。

底层逻辑 · (a) 为什么是这个分布 T4

① 给定 $x$ 和参数后，$f_\beta(x) = \beta^\top\phi(x)$ 是一个确定的数，记作 $c$。② 唯一的随机性来自 $\varepsilon\sim\mathcal N(0,\sigma^2)$。③ 常数加上随机变量：$\mathbb E[c+\varepsilon] = c + 0$，$\operatorname{var}(c + \varepsilon) = \operatorname{var}(\varepsilon) = \sigma^2$（2.5 的平移性质）；而高斯平移之后仍是高斯。所以 $y\mid x\sim\mathcal N(\beta^\top\phi(x),\sigma^2)$。写出密度最稳妥：

$$p(y\mid x) = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^2}}\exp\Big(-\frac{(y - \beta^\top\phi(x))^2}{2\sigma^2}\Big)$$
底层逻辑 · (b) 每一步的理由（10 分题，每一步都要写出来）

① 独立 ⇒ 乘积：在给定参数和输入的条件下各 $y_n$ 独立，联合密度等于边缘密度的乘积（2.7）：$p(y\mid X) = \prod_{n=1}^Np(y_n\mid x_n)$。

② 代入 (a)：每一项都是 $\mathcal N(y_n\mid\beta^\top\phi(x_n),\sigma^2)$ 的密度。

③ 指数的乘积 = 和的指数：$\prod_ne^{a_n} = e^{\sum_na_n}$；$N$ 个相同的常数 $\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^2}}$ 相乘得 $(2\pi\sigma^2)^{-N/2}$：

$$p(y\mid X) = \Big(\frac{1}{2\pi\sigma^2}\Big)^{N/2}\exp\Big(-\frac{1}{2\sigma^2}\sum_{n=1}^N\big(y_n - \beta^\top\phi(x_n)\big)^2\Big) = \mathcal N(y\mid\Phi\beta,\ \sigma^2I)$$

④ （加分）写成矩阵形式，再指出：取对数得 $-\frac N2\log2\pi\sigma^2 - \frac{1}{2\sigma^2}\|y - \Phi\beta\|^2$，对 $\beta$ 最大化就是最小二乘（3.4）。

满分答案模板 · Model answer

(a) Given $x$ and the parameters, $f_\beta(x) = \beta^\top\phi(x)$ is a fixed (non-random) number, so the only randomness in $y = f_\beta(x) + \varepsilon$ comes from $\varepsilon\sim\mathcal N(0,\sigma^2)$. Adding a constant to a Gaussian shifts its mean and leaves its variance unchanged, hence $y\mid x\sim\mathcal N(\beta^\top\phi(x),\sigma^2)$, i.e. $p(y\mid x) = (2\pi\sigma^2)^{-1/2}\exp\big(-(y-\beta^\top\phi(x))^2/(2\sigma^2)\big)$.

(b) Assume the observations are conditionally independent given the inputs and parameters, with i.i.d. noise. Then the joint density factorises: $p(y\mid X) = \prod_{n=1}^Np(y_n\mid x_n) = \prod_{n=1}^N\mathcal N(y_n\mid\beta^\top\phi(x_n),\sigma^2) = (2\pi\sigma^2)^{-N/2}\exp\big(-\frac{1}{2\sigma^2}\sum_{n=1}^N(y_n-\beta^\top\phi(x_n))^2\big)$, which is the multivariate Gaussian $\mathcal N(\Phi\beta,\sigma^2I)$, with $\Phi$ the design matrix. Viewed as a function of $\beta$ and $\sigma^2$, this is the likelihood.

注意 · 题目本身的记号冲突

题干同时写了 "$f_\beta(x) = \beta^T\phi(x)$"（$\beta$ 是权重）和 "$\beta$ is the noise precision"（$\beta$ 又是噪声精度），还提到一个没有出现的 "$\mathbf w$ is a vector of parameters"。这是把 Bishop 教材的记号（权重 $w$、精度 $\beta$）和课件的记号（权重 $\beta$、精度 $\eta$）混在了一起。答题时在开头声明一句："Let $\beta$ denote the weights and $\sigma^2 = 1/\eta$ the noise variance"，就不会被扣分。

2 In binary logistic regression, what link function is used and what is its inverse? Explain why we call it a link function. (5 marks)

官方答案：用 logit 链接，它的反函数是 logistic / sigmoid 函数；logit 也就是对数几率。逻辑回归是一个广义线性模型 $g(\mathbb E[Y\mid x]) = \eta(x)$，$\eta(x) = w^\top x + b$，链接函数 $g$ 把响应的条件均值 $\mathbb E[Y\mid x]$ 和线性预测器 $w^\top x + b$ 联系起来。

底层逻辑

"链接"的本质是值域不匹配：$\mathbb E[Y\mid x] = p(Y=1\mid x)\in(0,1)$，而线性预测器 $w^\top x + b\in\mathbb R$。logit $g(p) = \log\frac{p}{1-p}$ 把 $(0,1)$ 一一映射到 $\mathbb R$，于是可以令 $g(\mathbb E[Y\mid x]) = w^\top x + b$。反函数 $\sigma(\eta) = \frac{1}{1+e^{-\eta}}$ 把线性预测器映射回概率（推导见 5.3）。要拿满分，三样东西都要写出来：两个公式、GLM 方程、值域的理由。

满分答案模板 · Model answer

Logistic regression uses the logit link $g(p) = \log\frac{p}{1-p}$ (the log-odds), whose inverse is the logistic (sigmoid) function $\sigma(\eta) = \frac{1}{1+e^{-\eta}}$, so $p(Y=1\mid x) = \sigma(w^\top x + b)$. It is a generalised linear model, $g\big(\mathbb E[Y\mid x]\big) = w^\top x + b$. We call $g$ a link function because it links the conditional mean of the response, $\mathbb E[Y\mid x] = p(Y=1\mid x)\in(0,1)$, to the linear predictor $w^\top x + b$, which takes values in $\mathbb R$: the logit maps $(0,1)$ onto $\mathbb R$, the range of a linear predictor.

3 Write down the class probabilities for the softmax model. Explain why we need a reference class and why we need an identifiability constraint. (5 marks)

官方答案：$\mu_k(x) = \frac{e^{\theta_k^\top x}}{\sum_{j=1}^Ke^{\theta_j^\top x}}$；由全概率公式，$K$ 个概率不能独立指定（它们在 $K-1$ 维单纯形上），所以选参考类 $K$，$\mu_K = 1 - \sum_{k\lt K}\mu_k$；给所有线性判别函数加上任意函数 $c(x)$ 不改变 $\mu_k$，所以要加可辨识性约束（如 $\theta_K = 0$）来保证解唯一。

底层逻辑 · 两个"为什么"不是一回事，要分开答

参考类说的是概率：$K$ 个概率之和必须为 1，只有 $K-1$ 个是自由的，所以链接函数用"相对参考类的对数几率" $\log\frac{\mu_k}{\mu_K}$，只需要 $K-1$ 个。

可辨识性说的是参数：$\theta_k\mapsto\theta_k + v$（对所有 $k$ 加同一个 $v$）给出完全相同的概率，因为分子分母同乘 $e^{v^\top x}$ 会抵消。同一组概率对应无穷多组参数，最优解不唯一（Hessian 也是奇异的）。令 $\theta_K = 0$ 把这个自由度固定住。

很多人把两者混成一句话。分开写、各给一个理由，才能拿满 5 分。

满分答案模板 · Model answer

The softmax model gives $p(Y = k\mid x) = \mu_k(x) = \dfrac{\exp(\theta_k^\top x)}{\sum_{j=1}^K\exp(\theta_j^\top x)}$, $k = 1,\dots,K$. Reference class: by the law of total probability $\sum_k\mu_k(x) = 1$, so only $K-1$ of the probabilities can be specified independently (they lie on the $(K-1)$-simplex); we therefore choose a reference class, say $K$, set $\mu_K = 1 - \sum_{k\lt K}\mu_k$, and model the log-odds $\log(\mu_k/\mu_K) = \theta_k^\top x$ for $k = 1,\dots,K-1$. Identifiability: adding the same function $c(x)$ to every score, $\theta_k^\top x\mapsto\theta_k^\top x + c(x)$, leaves all $\mu_k$ unchanged, so the parameters are not uniquely determined by the probabilities; imposing a constraint such as $\theta_K = 0$ removes this freedom and makes the solution unique.

注意 · 官方答案的小问题

官方答案写 “adding any function $c(x)$ to the linear determinants”，应为 linear discriminants（或 scores）；"probabilities can be scaled arbitrarily" 更准确的说法是：得分可以被任意平移，而概率不变。

4 In the hard-margin linear SVM, classifying a new point requires $\operatorname{sign}(f(x)) = \operatorname{sign}\big(\sum_{i=1}^n\alpha_iy_ix^Tx_i + b\big)$. Explain what the support vectors are and hence why most of the terms in the argument of the sign function are zero. (4 marks)

官方答案：支持向量是落在 $b + w^\top x = \pm1$ 上的数据点，$w = \sum_i\alpha_iy_ix_i$。由 KKT 条件，对每个点，要么 $\alpha_i = 0$，要么 $y_i(b + w^\top x_i) - 1 = 0$；只有支持向量满足后者，所以大多数点 $\alpha_i = 0$，对求和没有贡献。

底层逻辑 T5

这道题的推理链有三环：① 对偶给出 $w = \sum_i\alpha_iy_ix_i$，所以 $f(x)$ 是对训练点求和；② 互补松弛 $\alpha_i\big(y_i(w^\top x_i + b) - 1\big) = 0$：两个非负数的乘积为 0，至少一个为 0；③ 大多数训练点离边界很远（$y_if(x_i)\gt1$），括号不为 0，于是 $\alpha_i = 0$。只有恰好落在间隔线上的点（支持向量）才可能 $\alpha_i\gt0$。直觉：约束不起作用，它的"价格"（乘子）就是 0（5.10）。

满分答案模板 · Model answer

The dual solution gives $w = \sum_i\alpha_iy_ix_i$ with Lagrange multipliers $\alpha_i\ge0$. The KKT complementary slackness condition states $\alpha_i\big(y_i(w^\top x_i + b) - 1\big) = 0$ for every $i$, so either $\alpha_i = 0$ or $y_i(w^\top x_i+b) = 1$. The support vectors are the training points with $\alpha_i\gt0$; they lie exactly on the margin hyperplanes $w^\top x + b = \pm1$. All other points lie strictly outside the margin, $y_i(w^\top x_i + b)\gt1$, which forces $\alpha_i = 0$. Since typically only a few points lie on the margin, most $\alpha_i$ are zero and the corresponding terms $\alpha_iy_ix^\top x_i$ vanish; the classifier depends only on the support vectors.

注意 · 官方答案的用词

官方答案说支持向量落在 “the separating hyperplanes defined by $b + w^\top x = \pm1$”。分隔超平面（决策边界）是 $w^\top x + b = 0$；$\pm1$ 的那两条叫间隔超平面 / 支撑超平面 (margin / support hyperplanes)。答题时用后一种说法。

5 In a PCA of a data set, the largest six eigenvalues of the covariance matrix are $1.2,\ 0.7,\ 0.2,\ 0.05,\ 0.01,\ 0.001$ and $\sum_i\lambda_i = 2.216$. How many principal directions must be included to capture at least 95% of the generalised variance (write down the relevant formula)? (3 marks)

官方答案：需要 4 个，因为前 3 个只占 $0.9477$，前 4 个占 $0.9702$。

PCA 速成 · 课件里还没有，这里讲到够答题 T7

PCA（主成分分析，属于后面的降维部分）找的是数据方差最大的一组正交方向。做法：求数据的协方差矩阵 $C = \frac1n\sum_i(x_i-\bar x)(x_i-\bar x)^\top$（2.9），做特征分解 $C = U\Lambda U^\top$。特征向量 $u_j$ 是主方向，特征值 $\lambda_j$ 是数据投影到这个方向上的方差。

为什么特征值就是方差？数据投影到单位向量 $u_j$ 上得到 $u_j^\top x$，它的方差是 $u_j^\top Cu_j = u_j^\top(\lambda_ju_j) = \lambda_j$（用了 $Cu_j = \lambda_ju_j$ 和 $u_j^\top u_j = 1$）。

总（广义）方差是 $\operatorname{tr}(C) = \sum_j\lambda_j$（迹 = 特征值之和 = 各坐标方差之和）。前 $k$ 个主方向保留的比例是

$$\frac{\sum_{i=1}^k\lambda_i}{\sum_{i}\lambda_i}$$

几何上：2.10 交互图里那两条橙色的椭圆主轴，就是二维数据的两个主方向。

计算
k前 k 个之和比例
11.254.2%
21.985.7%
32.194.77% < 95%
42.1597.02% ≥ 95%

所以需要 4 个主方向。

陷阱：列出的六个特征值之和是 2.161，不是 2.216，说明还有更小的特征值没列出（合计 0.055）。分母必须用题目给的总和 2.216。如果用 2.161 当分母，前 3 个的比例是 $2.1/2.161 = 97.2\%$，就会错选 3。

满分答案模板 · Model answer

The eigenvalue $\lambda_i$ of the covariance matrix is the variance of the data along the $i$-th principal direction, and the total (generalised) variance is $\sum_i\lambda_i = 2.216$. The fraction captured by the first $k$ directions is $\sum_{i=1}^k\lambda_i\big/\sum_i\lambda_i$. For $k = 3$: $(1.2+0.7+0.2)/2.216 = 0.948\lt0.95$; for $k = 4$: $2.15/2.216 = 0.970\ge0.95$. Hence 4 principal directions are required.

注意 · 记号

题目写 $\sum_{i=1}^n\lambda_i$，这里 $n$ 是数据点个数。但特征值的个数等于数据的维数（协方差矩阵是 $d\times d$），和 $n$ 无关。官方答案里又写成 $\sum_{i=1}^N$。不影响计算，但自己答题时写 $\sum_{i=1}^d$ 更准确。

6 What is the risk of a model in supervised learning and why can we not minimize the risk? What is empirical risk minimization? (8 marks)

官方答案：用损失 $\ell(y, f(x))$ 衡量预测与目标的差异；风险是期望损失 $\mathcal R(f) = \mathbb E[\ell(Y, f(X))]$，期望对真实分布 $P(X,Y)$ 取。但这个分布未知，而且无法搜索所有可能的函数。于是从某个假设类 $\mathcal H$ 中取依赖参数 $\beta$ 的模型 $f_\beta$，用数据的蒙特卡洛估计（经验风险）$\hat{\mathcal R}(f_\beta) = \frac1n\sum_i\ell(y_i, f_\beta(x_i))$ 代替风险，并求解 $\operatorname{argmin}_\beta\hat{\mathcal R}(f_\beta)$。

底层逻辑 · 两个障碍、两个对策（8 分题按这个结构答）

障碍 1：风险是对 $P(X,Y)$ 的期望，而 $P(X,Y)$ 未知，所以根本算不出来。对策：用样本平均代替期望（蒙特卡洛估计）。由大数定律，样本越多，经验风险越接近真实风险。

障碍 2：要在所有函数 $f:\mathcal X\to\mathcal Y$ 里找最优，这既做不到，也没有意义（任何穿过所有数据点的函数经验风险都是 0）。对策：限制到一个假设类 $\mathcal H = \{f_\beta\}$，优化有限个（或受控的）参数。

加分点：平方损失下，风险的最小化者是条件均值 $\mathbb E[Y\mid X = x]$（Bayes 预测器，3.1）；平方损失 + 线性模型的 ERM 就是 OLS；在高斯噪声下它又等于 MLE（3.4）。

满分答案模板 · Model answer

A loss function $\ell(y, f(x))$ measures the discrepancy between the prediction $f(x)$ and the target $y$. The risk of a predictor $f$ is its expected loss, $\mathcal R(f) = \mathbb E\big[\ell(Y, f(X))\big]$, where the expectation is taken with respect to the true joint distribution $P(X,Y)$; its minimiser is the Bayes predictor, which for the square loss is the conditional mean $\mathbb E[Y\mid X = x]$. We cannot minimise the risk because $P(X,Y)$ is unknown, so the expectation cannot be evaluated, and because we cannot search over all possible functions $f:\mathcal X\to\mathcal Y$. In empirical risk minimisation we instead choose a model $f_\beta$ from a hypothesis class $\mathcal H$ and replace the risk by its Monte Carlo estimate from the data, the empirical risk $\hat{\mathcal R}(f_\beta) = \frac1n\sum_{i=1}^n\ell(y_i, f_\beta(x_i))$, and solve $\operatorname{argmin}_\beta\hat{\mathcal R}(f_\beta)$. For the square loss and a linear model this is ordinary least squares.

答题方法总结
  • 简答题三段式：一句话定义 → 写出公式 → 用"because…"说明为什么。官方答案的给分点几乎都在"为什么"那一段。
  • 推导题每一步都写理由：例如 “by independence the joint density factorises”、“taking logs turns the product into a sum”。分数是按步骤给的。
  • 记号：用课件的记号；题目记号冲突时，开头用一句话声明你的记号。
  • 计算题：先写公式，再代数，最后写一句结论；注意题目给的数据（例如 PCA 的总和）是不是"完整的"。
Practice · Parts 1–5

期末模拟题

覆盖五份课件。题干用英文，和考试一样；解答用中文，关键术语附英文。先在纸上完整做一遍，再展开对照。所有数字都用代码验算过。

20 题 · 完整解答

P1计算与证明 · Calculations and proofs

Part 1–2：回归与概率

1 Fit $y = ax + b$ by least squares to the data $(x, y) = (-1, 0),\ (0, 1),\ (1, 1),\ (2, 3)$ using the normal equations. Report $a$, $b$ and the total square error $E$.

设计矩阵每行为 $(x_i, 1)$：

$$X^\top X = \begin{pmatrix}\sum x_i^2 & \sum x_i\\ \sum x_i & N\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}6 & 2\\ 2 & 4\end{pmatrix},\qquad X^\top y = \begin{pmatrix}\sum x_iy_i\\ \sum y_i\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}7\\5\end{pmatrix}$$ $$\theta = (X^\top X)^{-1}X^\top y = \frac{1}{20}\begin{pmatrix}4 & -2\\ -2 & 6\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}7\\5\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}0.9\\0.8\end{pmatrix}$$

所以 $a = 0.9$，$b = 0.8$。用 1.2 的公式验算：$\bar x = 0.5$，$\bar y = 1.25$，$S_{xy} = 4.5$，$S_{xx} = 5$，$a = 0.9$，$b = 1.25 - 0.45 = 0.8$，一致。

残差 $y_i - (ax_i + b)$ 为 $0.1,\ 0.2,\ -0.7,\ 0.4$，所以 $E = 0.01 + 0.04 + 0.49 + 0.16 = 0.70$。注意残差之和为 0：只要模型带截距，$\partial E/\partial b = 0$ 就保证了这一点，可以用来快速检查答案。

2 $X, Y$ have joint p.d.f. $p(x,y) = x + y$ on $[0,1]^2$ (zero elsewhere). (a) Show it is a valid p.d.f. (b) Find the marginal $p(x)$ and the conditional $p(y\mid x)$. (c) Compute $\mathbb E[X]$, $\operatorname{var}(X)$ and $\operatorname{cov}(X, Y)$. (d) Are $X$ and $Y$ independent?

(a) 非负，且 $\int_0^1\!\int_0^1 (x+y)\,dx\,dy = \frac12 + \frac12 = 1$。

(b) 边缘：对 $y$ 积分，$p(x) = \int_0^1(x+y)\,dy = x + \frac12$。条件：$p(y\mid x) = \dfrac{p(x,y)}{p(x)} = \dfrac{x+y}{x+\frac12}$。检查：对 $y$ 积分得 $\frac{x + 1/2}{x+1/2} = 1$。

(c) $\mathbb E[X] = \int_0^1 x(x+\tfrac12)\,dx = \frac13 + \frac14 = \frac{7}{12}$；$\mathbb E[X^2] = \int_0^1x^2(x+\tfrac12)\,dx = \frac14 + \frac16 = \frac5{12}$；

$$\operatorname{var}(X) = \frac{5}{12} - \Big(\frac{7}{12}\Big)^2 = \frac{60 - 49}{144} = \frac{11}{144}$$

由对称性，$\mathbb E[Y] = \frac7{12}$。$\mathbb E[XY] = \iint xy(x+y) = \iint (x^2y + xy^2) = \frac16 + \frac16 = \frac13$，所以

$$\operatorname{cov}(X,Y) = \frac13 - \frac{49}{144} = -\frac{1}{144},\qquad \rho = \frac{-1/144}{11/144} = -\frac1{11}$$

(d) 不独立。$p(x)p(y) = (x+\frac12)(y+\frac12) \ne x + y$，例如在 $(0,0)$ 处分别是 $\frac14$ 和 $0$。协方差不为 0 本身就说明了不独立。

3 $X$ is uniform on $\{-1, 0, 1\}$ and $Y = X^2$. Show that $\operatorname{cov}(X, Y) = 0$ but $X$ and $Y$ are not independent.

$\mathbb E[X] = 0$，$\mathbb E[XY] = \mathbb E[X^3] = \frac{(-1) + 0 + 1}{3} = 0$，所以 $\operatorname{cov}(X,Y) = 0 - 0\cdot\mathbb E[Y] = 0$。

但 $\mathbb P(X = 0, Y = 0) = \mathbb P(X = 0) = \frac13$，而 $\mathbb P(X=0)\,\mathbb P(Y = 0) = \frac13\cdot\frac13 = \frac19$，两者不等，所以不独立。这是 2.8 那个连续反例的离散版本。

4 Show that $\operatorname{cov}(A\mathbf X + \mathbf b) = A\operatorname{cov}(\mathbf X)A^\top$ for a constant matrix $A$ and vector $\mathbf b$. If $\mathbf X\sim\mathcal N(\mathbf 0, I_2)$ and $A = \begin{pmatrix}1 & 0\\ 1 & 1\end{pmatrix}$, find the covariance matrix and correlation coefficient of $\mathbf Y = A\mathbf X$ and describe its contours.

证明：$\mathbb E[\mathbf Y] = A\boldsymbol\mu + \mathbf b$，所以 $\mathbf Y - \mathbb E[\mathbf Y] = A(\mathbf X - \boldsymbol\mu)$，

$$\operatorname{cov}(\mathbf Y) = \mathbb E\big[A(\mathbf X-\boldsymbol\mu)(\mathbf X-\boldsymbol\mu)^\top A^\top\big] = A\,\mathbb E\big[(\mathbf X-\boldsymbol\mu)(\mathbf X-\boldsymbol\mu)^\top\big]A^\top = A\operatorname{cov}(\mathbf X)A^\top$$

常数矩阵可以提到期望外面，平移 $\mathbf b$ 不影响协方差。

计算：$\boldsymbol\Sigma_Y = AIA^\top = AA^\top = \begin{pmatrix}1 & 1\\ 1 & 2\end{pmatrix}$。$\operatorname{var}(Y_1) = 1$，$\operatorname{var}(Y_2) = 2$，$\operatorname{cov} = 1$，所以 $\rho = \frac{1}{\sqrt{1\cdot2}} \approx 0.707$。

等高线：高斯向量的线性变换仍是高斯的。特征值为 $\frac{3\pm\sqrt5}{2} \approx 2.618,\ 0.382$，长轴沿特征向量 $(1, 1.618)$，与 $x_1$ 轴约成 58°，是倾斜的椭圆，长短轴之比 $\sqrt{2.618/0.382}\approx 2.6$。

这正是 2.10 交互图生成样本的方法：取 $\mathbf z\sim\mathcal N(\mathbf 0, I)$，令 $\mathbf x = L\mathbf z$，其中 $LL^\top = \boldsymbol\Sigma$（Cholesky 分解）。本题的 $A$ 就是 $\boldsymbol\Sigma_Y$ 的 Cholesky 因子。

5 $X\sim\mathcal N(2, 9)$. Estimate (a) $\mathbb P(X \gt 8)$; (b) $\mathbb P(-1\le X\le 5)$. (c) What is the distribution of $Z = (X - 2)/3$?

先看清参数：$\mathcal N(\mu, \sigma^2)$ 的第二个参数是方差，所以 $\sigma = 3$，不是 9。这是最常见的失分点。

(a) $8 = \mu + 2\sigma$。高斯分布约 95.4% 落在 $\pm2\sigma$ 内，剩下的 4.6% 两侧对称平分，所以 $\mathbb P(X \gt 8)\approx 2.3\%$。

(b) $[-1, 5] = [\mu - \sigma, \mu+\sigma]$，概率 $\approx 68.3\%$。

(c) 由 $\mathbb E[aX+b] = a\mathbb E[X]+b$ 和 $\operatorname{var}(aX+b) = a^2\operatorname{var}(X)$：$\mathbb E[Z] = 0$，$\operatorname{var}(Z) = 9/9 = 1$；高斯的线性变换仍是高斯，所以 $Z\sim\mathcal N(0,1)$。

6 A sensor alarm is modelled as $X\sim\operatorname{Bern}(\mu)$. In 20 independent trials it fires 6 times. Write down the log-likelihood, find the maximum-likelihood estimate $\hat\mu$, and estimate $\operatorname{var}(X)$.

独立，所以似然是乘积：$\mathcal L(\mu) = \prod_i\mu^{x_i}(1-\mu)^{1-x_i} = \mu^6(1-\mu)^{14}$，于是

$$\log\mathcal L(\mu) = 6\log\mu + 14\log(1-\mu),\qquad \frac{d\log\mathcal L}{d\mu} = \frac6\mu - \frac{14}{1-\mu} = 0\ \Rightarrow\ \hat\mu = \frac{6}{20} = 0.3$$

二阶导数 $-\frac6{\mu^2} - \frac{14}{(1-\mu)^2} \lt 0$，确实是最大值。方差估计为 $\hat\mu(1-\hat\mu) = 0.3\times0.7 = 0.21$。

Part 3：参数回归

7 A constant model $f(x) = \beta_0$ is fitted by least squares to $y = (1, 2, 6)^\top$. (a) Find the hat matrix $H$ and the leverages $H_{nn}$. (b) Compute LOOCV with the hat-matrix formula and check it by actually leaving each point out. (c) Compute GCV and the training MSE.

(a) $\Phi = (1,1,1)^\top$，$\Phi^\top\Phi = 3$，$H = \Phi(\Phi^\top\Phi)^{-1}\Phi^\top = \frac13\mathbf 1\mathbf 1^\top$，每个 $H_{nn} = \frac13$，$\operatorname{tr}H = 1$（一个参数）。拟合值 $\hat y_n = \bar y = 3$。

(b) 残差 $(-2, -1, 3)$，除以 $1 - \frac13 = \frac23$ 得留一残差 $(-3, -1.5, 4.5)$，所以 $\text{LOOCV} = \frac{9 + 2.25 + 20.25}{3} = 10.5$。直接验证：去掉第 1 个点，均值是 4，误差 $1 - 4 = -3$；去掉第 2 个，均值 3.5，误差 $-1.5$；去掉第 3 个，均值 1.5，误差 $4.5$。完全一致。

(c) 训练 MSE $= \frac{4+1+9}{3} = \frac{14}{3}\approx4.67$；$\text{GCV} = \frac{14/3}{(1 - 1/3)^2} = 10.5$。所有杠杆值相等，所以 GCV 和 LOOCV 恰好相同。LOOCV 是训练误差的两倍多：训练误差明显低估了预测误差。

8 For an orthonormal design ($\Phi^\top\Phi = I$) the least-squares coefficients are $b = \Phi^\top y = (3,\ -0.5,\ 1.2)^\top$. With $E(\beta) = \frac12\|y - \Phi\beta\|^2$ and $\lambda = 1$, compute (a) the ridge solution and (b) the LASSO solution. (c) Which coefficients does LASSO remove?

正交设计下目标函数可分离（3.6 的推导）。(a) 岭回归 $\beta_i = b_i/(1+\lambda) = (1.5,\ -0.25,\ 0.6)$。(b) LASSO 软阈值 $\beta_i = \operatorname{sign}(b_i)\max(|b_i| - 1, 0) = (2,\ 0,\ 0.2)$。(c) 第二个系数 $|b_2| = 0.5\le\lambda$，被置为 0。岭回归按比例缩小所有系数；LASSO 把每个系数向 0 平移 λ，碰到 0 就停在 0。

9 Bayesian linear regression with one feature $\phi(x) = x$, prior $\beta\sim\mathcal N(0, \alpha^{-1})$ with $\alpha = 1$, noise precision $\eta = 4$, and data $x = (1, 2)^\top$, $y = (1, 3)^\top$. (a) Find the posterior mean $m$ and variance $S$. (b) Compare $m$ with the OLS estimate and with ridge regression for a suitable $\lambda$. (c) Give the predictive mean and variance at $x_* = 3$.

(a) $\Phi^\top\Phi = \sum x_n^2 = 5$，$\Phi^\top y = \sum x_ny_n = 7$。$S = (\alpha + \eta\Phi^\top\Phi)^{-1} = \frac{1}{1 + 20} = \frac1{21}$，$m = \eta S\Phi^\top y = \frac{4\times7}{21} = \frac43\approx1.333$。

(b) OLS：$\beta_{\text{ML}} = 7/5 = 1.4$。后验均值被先验往 0 拉了一点。岭回归取 $\lambda = \alpha/\eta = 0.25$：$\beta = \frac{7}{5 + 0.25} = \frac43$，与 MAP（高斯后验的均值 = 众数）完全相同。

(c) 均值 $m\,x_* = 4$；方差 $\eta^{-1} + x_*^2S = 0.25 + \frac{9}{21}\approx0.679$，标准差约 0.82。其中 0.25 来自噪声，0.43 来自参数的不确定性。

10 Data $x = (0, 1, 2)$, $y = (0, 2, 4)$ lie exactly on $y = 2x$. At the boundary point $x_* = 0$ the kernel weights are $w = (1,\ 0.5,\ 0.1)$. Compute the Nadaraya–Watson and the local linear estimates of $f(0)$, and explain the difference.

NW：$\hat f = \frac{\sum w_ny_n}{\sum w_n} = \frac{0 + 1 + 0.4}{1.6} = 0.875$，而真实值是 0。

局部线性：$d_n = x_n - x_* = (0, 1, 2)$。$S_0 = 1.6$，$S_1 = \sum w_nd_n = 0.7$，$S_2 = \sum w_nd_n^2 = 0.9$，$T_0 = \sum w_ny_n = 1.4$，$T_1 = \sum w_nd_ny_n = 1.8$。行列式 $S_0S_2 - S_1^2 = 0.95$，

$$\hat\beta_0 = \frac{S_2T_0 - S_1T_1}{0.95} = \frac{1.26 - 1.26}{0.95} = 0,\qquad \hat\beta_1 = \frac{S_0T_1 - S_1T_0}{0.95} = \frac{2.88 - 0.98}{0.95} = 2$$

局部线性给出精确的 $f(0) = 0$ 和斜率 2。NW 在边界只能对右边那些更高的点取平均，所以偏高，这就是边界偏差；局部线性多拟合了一个斜率，能精确重现直线。

Part 4：核方法与神经网络

11 (a) Show that if $k_1, k_2$ are valid kernels then so are $k_1 + k_2$ and $c\,k_1$ for $c \gt 0$. (b) Show that $k(x,x') = g(x)\,g(x')$ is a valid kernel for any real function $g$. (c) Show that $d(x,x') = \|x - x'\|^2$ is not a valid kernel.

合法核 = 对称 + 任意 Gram 矩阵半正定。逐条验证：

(a) Gram 矩阵分别是 $K_1 + K_2$ 和 $cK_1$，仍然对称；对任意 $v$，$v^\top(K_1+K_2)v = v^\top K_1v + v^\top K_2v\ge0$，$v^\top(cK_1)v = c\,v^\top K_1v\ge0$。

(b) 对称性显然。$\sum_{i,j}v_iv_j\,g(x_i)g(x_j) = \big(\sum_iv_ig(x_i)\big)^2\ge0$。对应的特征映射是一维的 $\psi(x) = g(x)$。

(c) 取两个不同的点，距离为 $d \gt 0$。Gram 矩阵为 $\begin{pmatrix}0 & d^2\\ d^2 & 0\end{pmatrix}$，取 $v = (1, -1)^\top$，得 $v^\top Kv = -2d^2 \lt 0$，不是半正定的。直觉上，核衡量的是相似度，距离恰好相反。高斯核 $e^{-\|x-x'\|^2/\theta}$ 正是把距离转换成了相似度。

补充：两个核的乘积也是核（Schur 积定理），所以可以用加法和乘法从简单的核拼出复杂的核。

12 Data $x = (0, 1)^\top$, $y = (1, 3)^\top$, features $\phi(x) = (1, x)^\top$ so that $k(x,x') = 1 + xx'$, and $\lambda = 1$. (a) Compute $K$, $\alpha = (K + \lambda I)^{-1}y$ and $f(x) = k(x)^\top\alpha$. (b) Compute the primal ridge solution $\beta = (\Phi^\top\Phi + \lambda I)^{-1}\Phi^\top y$ and check both give the same $f$. (c) Predict at $x = 2$.

(a) $K = \begin{pmatrix}k(0,0) & k(0,1)\\ k(1,0) & k(1,1)\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}1 & 1\\ 1 & 2\end{pmatrix}$，$K + I = \begin{pmatrix}2 & 1\\ 1 & 3\end{pmatrix}$，行列式为 5。

$$\alpha = \frac15\begin{pmatrix}3 & -1\\ -1 & 2\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}1\\3\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}0\\1\end{pmatrix},\qquad f(x) = 0\cdot k(x, 0) + 1\cdot k(x, 1) = 1 + x$$

(b) $\Phi = \begin{pmatrix}1 & 0\\ 1 & 1\end{pmatrix}$，$\Phi^\top\Phi + I = \begin{pmatrix}3 & 1\\ 1 & 2\end{pmatrix}$，$\Phi^\top y = (4, 3)^\top$，

$$\beta = \frac15\begin{pmatrix}2 & -1\\ -1 & 3\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}4\\3\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}1\\1\end{pmatrix},\qquad f(x) = \beta^\top\phi(x) = 1 + x$$

原问题和对偶问题给出同一个函数，正是 4.6 的结论。(c) $f(2) = 3$。

值得注意：不加正则时，直线会精确穿过两点：$y = 1 + 2x$。岭回归把斜率从 2 收缩到了 1，因为惩罚 $\lambda\|\beta\|^2$ 把系数往 0 拉。这里的 $\alpha$ 用的是 $(K+\lambda I)^{-1}y$ 这种约定，见 4.6 的勘误框。

13 For two training points the Gram matrix is $K = \begin{pmatrix}1 & 0.5\\ 0.5 & 1\end{pmatrix}$, $y = (1, -1)^\top$ and $\lambda = 0.5$. For a test point $x_*$, $k(x_*) = (0.8,\ 0.2)^\top$. Compute the KRR prediction $f(x_*)$.

$K + \lambda I = \begin{pmatrix}1.5 & 0.5\\ 0.5 & 1.5\end{pmatrix}$，行列式 $= 2.25 - 0.25 = 2$，

$$\alpha = \frac12\begin{pmatrix}1.5 & -0.5\\ -0.5 & 1.5\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}1\\-1\end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix}1\\-1\end{pmatrix},\qquad f(x_*) = k(x_*)^\top\alpha = 0.8 - 0.2 = 0.6$$

检查：$(K+\lambda I)\alpha = (1.5 - 0.5,\ 0.5 - 1.5)^\top = (1, -1)^\top = y$。直觉：$x_*$ 和第一个点更相似（0.8 对 0.2），所以预测偏向第一个标签 $+1$。

14 A one-hidden-layer network with $d = d_1 = 1$ uses the logistic sigmoid $\sigma(a) = 1/(1 + e^{-a})$, with $W = 0.5$, $b = -1$, $v = 2$, $c = 0$. For the sample $(x, y) = (2, 0)$ and loss $\ell = \frac12(\hat y - y)^2$: (a) do the forward pass; (b) compute all four gradients by backpropagation; (c) take one SGD step with $\eta = 0.5$ and show that the loss decreases. Hint: $\sigma'(a) = \sigma(a)\big(1 - \sigma(a)\big)$.
(a) 前向$a = 0.5\times2 - 1 = 0$，$z = \sigma(0) = 0.5$，$\hat y = 2\times0.5 + 0 = 1$，$\ell = \frac12(1 - 0)^2 = 0.5$
输出误差$\delta^{(o)} = \hat y - y = 1$
输出层梯度$\partial\ell/\partial v = \delta^{(o)}z = 0.5$，$\partial\ell/\partial c = \delta^{(o)} = 1$
隐藏层误差$\sigma'(0) = 0.5\times0.5 = 0.25$，$\delta^{(h)} = \delta^{(o)}\,v\,\sigma'(a) = 1\times2\times0.25 = 0.5$
隐藏层梯度$\partial\ell/\partial W = \delta^{(h)}x = 1$，$\partial\ell/\partial b = \delta^{(h)} = 0.5$
(c) 更新$v = 2 - 0.25 = 1.75$，$c = -0.5$，$W = 0.5 - 0.5 = 0$，$b = -1 - 0.25 = -1.25$
再前向$a = -1.25$，$z = \sigma(-1.25)\approx0.2227$，$\hat y \approx 1.75\times0.2227 - 0.5 \approx -0.110$，$\ell\approx0.0061$

损失从 0.5 降到约 0.006。注意要先算完全部梯度再统一更新：$\delta^{(h)}$ 用的是更新前的 $v = 2$。

15 $n = 1000$ training samples, mini-batch size $|B| = 32$, trained for 10 epochs. (a) How many parameter updates are made? (b) What is $\mathbb P(i\in B)$ for a uniformly sampled mini-batch? (c) How many updates would full-batch gradient descent make in 10 epochs, and why can SGD reach a lower loss for the same computation?

(a) 每个 epoch 有 $\lceil 1000/32\rceil = 32$ 个小批量（31 个满批，最后一批 8 个样本），共 $32\times10 = 320$ 次更新。

(b) $\mathbb P(i\in B) = |B|/n = 32/1000 = 0.032$。这正是无偏性证明里用到的量。

(c) 全批量每个 epoch 只更新 1 次，共 10 次。两者的计算量相同（都把每个样本的梯度算了 10 次），但 SGD 走了 32 倍多的步数。每一步虽然有噪声，但方向在期望上是对的，所以通常下降得快得多；噪声还有助于跳出鞍点。

16 A nonparametric estimator attains the minimax rate for $s$-Hölder functions in $d$ dimensions. With $N = 1000$, $s = 1$, $d = 2$, its mean squared error is $0.04$. (a) Roughly how many samples are needed for an MSE of $0.01$? (b) Repeat for $d = 20$. (c) How many would a correctly specified parametric model (rate $N^{-1}$) need?

(a) 速率 $N^{-2s/(2s+d)} = N^{-2/4} = N^{-1/2}$。误差要缩小到 1/4，$N$ 需要乘以 $4^2 = 16$，约 16,000。

(b) 速率 $N^{-2/22} = N^{-1/11}$。$N$ 需要乘以 $4^{11}\approx4.2\times10^6$，约 $4.2\times10^9$，完全不现实，这就是维数灾难。

(c) 速率 $N^{-1}$，只需乘以 4，约 4,000。前提是模型设定正确；设定错误时，误差会停在近似误差上不再下降（4.1）。

Part 5：分类

17 A logistic regression model has $w = (2, -1)^\top$ and $b = 0.5$. For $x = (1, 1)^\top$ compute the log-odds, the odds and $p(Y = 1\mid x)$, and give the predicted class. Write down the decision boundary.

对数几率 $= w^\top x + b = 2 - 1 + 0.5 = 1.5$；几率 $= e^{1.5}\approx4.48$；$p = \sigma(1.5) = \frac{1}{1 + e^{-1.5}}\approx0.818$；对数几率大于 0，预测为类 1。

决策边界是对数几率为 0 的地方：$2x_1 - x_2 + 0.5 = 0$，即直线 $x_2 = 2x_1 + 0.5$。

为什么不用先算出 $p$ 再和 0.5 比较？$p\gt0.5\iff$ 对数几率 $\gt0$，直接看 $w^\top x + b$ 的符号就够了（5.5）。

18 For the hyperplane $w^\top x + b = 0$ with $w = (3, 4)^\top$ and $b = -5$: (a) find the signed distance of $x = (2, 1)^\top$ from the hyperplane; (b) if this point has label $y = -1$, what is its geometric margin, and is it classified correctly? (c) If $(w, b)$ is an SVM solution in canonical form, what is the width of the margin?

(a) $f(x) = 6 + 4 - 5 = 5$，$\|w\| = \sqrt{9 + 16} = 5$，有符号距离 $d = f(x)/\|w\| = 1$（在 $w$ 指向的一侧）。

(b) $\gamma = y\,d = -1\lt0$：分错了，而且离边界的距离是 1。

(c) 标准形式下两条间隔线 $w^\top x + b = \pm1$ 相距 $2/\|w\| = 0.4$。

19 A three-class softmax model gives scores $\theta_k^\top x = (2, 1, 0)$. (a) Compute the class probabilities. (b) Show that adding 1 to every score leaves them unchanged. (c) If the true class is 1, what is the multiclass cross-entropy loss for this sample?

(a) $e^2 + e^1 + e^0 = 7.389 + 2.718 + 1 = 11.107$，概率为 $(0.665,\ 0.245,\ 0.090)$。

(b) 每个分子和分母都多乘了一个 $e^1$：$\frac{e\cdot e^{s_k}}{e\cdot\sum_je^{s_j}}$，约掉之后不变。这就是可辨识性问题（5.6）。

(c) 只有真实类那一项起作用：$-\log0.665 = 0.408$。

20 A soft-margin SVM has $w = (1, 0)^\top$, $b = 0$ and $C = 1$. The training points are $((2, 0), +1)$, $((0.5, 1), +1)$, $((-0.5, 0), +1)$ and $((-1, 1), -1)$. Compute each slack $\xi_i$, say which points are inside the margin or misclassified, and evaluate the primal objective.

$f(x) = x_1$，$\xi_i = \max(0,\ 1 - y_if(x_i))$：

点 1$y f = 2$ → $\xi = 0$：在间隔外，分对
点 2$y f = 0.5$ → $\xi = 0.5$：在间隔内，但仍分对
点 3$y f = -0.5$ → $\xi = 1.5$：分错（$\xi\gt1$）
点 4$y f = (-1)(-1) = 1$ → $\xi = 0$：正好在间隔线上

目标 $= \frac12\|w\|^2 + C\sum_i\xi_i = 0.5 + 1\times(0 + 0.5 + 1.5 + 0) = 2.5$。

为什么点 2、3 一定是支持向量？$\xi_i\gt0$ 的点，KKT 条件给出 $\alpha_i = C\gt0$（5.10）。点 4 在间隔线上，可能有 $0\lt\alpha\lt C$。点 1 在间隔外，$\alpha = 0$。（这里的 $w, b$ 只是给定的一组值，不一定是最优解。）

12 题 · 英文参考答案

P2英文简答 · Short answers in English

期末要用英文作答。答题顺序建议：先给定义，再写公式，最后举例或说明意义。下面的参考答案按这个结构写，每题附中文要点，方便你检查自己有没有漏掉关键点。

1 Distinguish parametric and nonparametric regression in the modern sense, with an example of each.

Parametric regression restricts the regression function to a hypothesis class indexed by a fixed, finite-dimensional parameter vector $\beta$, for example polynomial regression $f(x) = \beta^\top\phi(x)$; its complexity does not grow with the number of data points $n$. Nonparametric regression uses an infinite-dimensional hypothesis class, such as the RKHS in kernel ridge regression or Gaussian process regression, so its effective complexity grows with $n$: kernel ridge regression has one coefficient per data point. A correctly specified parametric model converges faster (rate $N^{-1}$) but suffers from model misspecification; a nonparametric model avoids misspecification at the cost of the slower rate $N^{-2s/(2s+d)}$.

要点：有限维 vs 无穷维；复杂度是否随 n 增长；各举一例；速率与设定错误之间的取舍。

2 Why is probability used in machine learning even when the data come from deterministic physical laws?

Even when the underlying process is deterministic, measurements contain noise and we only ever observe a finite data set, so the true relationship can never be known exactly. Modelling the data with random variables, e.g. $y = f(x) + \varepsilon$, lets us quantify this uncertainty and choose the most likely model given the data. For example, if the noise is i.i.d. Gaussian, maximising the likelihood is equivalent to minimising the sum of squared errors, which justifies least squares.

要点：有限数据 + 测量噪声；用概率量化不确定性、找"最可能"的模型；高斯噪声 ⇔ 最小二乘。

3 Explain kernel substitution (the kernel trick) and why it is useful.

The dual form of ridge regression depends on the inputs only through dot products $\phi(x)^\top\phi(x')$, collected in the Gram matrix $K$. Kernel substitution replaces these dot products by a kernel $k(x,x')$, a symmetric positive semi-definite function, which implicitly defines a feature map $\psi$ with $k(x,x') = \psi(x)^\top\psi(x')$. This performs linear ridge regression in a high- or even infinite-dimensional feature space, giving the nonlinear model $f(x) = k(x)^\top(K+\lambda I)^{-1}y$, without ever computing $\psi$. The cost depends on the number of data points $N$ (solving an $N\times N$ system) rather than on the feature dimension.

要点：对偶形式只含内积 → 换成核 → 隐式特征映射 → 非线性模型；计算量取决于 N 而不是特征维度。

4 State the representer theorem and explain its significance.

Let $\mathcal H_k$ be a RKHS with reproducing kernel $k$. For any loss $\ell$ and any strictly increasing regulariser $\Omega$, every minimiser of $\sum_{i=1}^n\ell(y_i, f(x_i)) + \Omega(\|f\|_{\mathcal H_k})$ over $f\in\mathcal H_k$ has the form $\hat f(x) = \sum_{i=1}^n\alpha_i k(x_i, x)$. Although $\mathcal H_k$ may be infinite-dimensional, the optimal solution lies in the finite span of the kernel sections at the data points, so the problem collapses to learning $n$ coefficients. This is the mathematical foundation of kernel ridge regression, support vector machines and Gaussian process regression.

要点：定理条件（RKHS、任意损失、严格递增的 Ω）；结论的形式；无穷维问题坍缩为 n 个系数；举出 KRR / SVM / GP。

5 Explain the bias–variance tradeoff using kernel ridge regression as an example.

For a fixed input $x$, the expected squared error of an estimator over random training sets decomposes as $\mathbb E[(\hat f(x) - f(x))^2] = (\mathbb E[\hat f(x)] - f(x))^2 + \mathbb E[(\hat f(x) - \mathbb E[\hat f(x)])^2]$, i.e. squared bias plus variance. In kernel ridge regression, a small lengthscale $\ell$ or a small regularisation parameter $\lambda$ gives a very flexible model with low bias but high variance: the fit follows the noise and changes a lot between data sets (overfitting). A large $\ell$ or $\lambda$ gives a smooth, rigid model with low variance but high bias (underfitting). The hyperparameters are chosen to balance the two, for example by grid-search cross-validation.

要点：写出分解公式；说明 ℓ、λ 小 → 过拟合，大 → 欠拟合；用交叉验证选超参数。

6 Why is stochastic gradient descent preferred to full gradient descent for large data sets? Is the mini-batch gradient biased?

Each full-gradient step requires a pass over all $n$ samples, which is expensive in time and memory when $n$ is large. SGD estimates the gradient from a random mini-batch $B$, so each update is much cheaper and many more updates can be made for the same cost. The mini-batch gradient is unbiased: since $\mathbb P(i\in B) = |B|/n$ under uniform sampling, $\mathbb E[\nabla_\theta\mathcal R_B] = \nabla_\theta\mathcal R$. It is, however, noisy, and this noise can help the optimiser escape shallow local minima and saddle points.

要点：全梯度代价随 n 增长；小批量更便宜、更新更频繁；无偏（给出期望的推导）；噪声的好处。

7 Explain why independence implies zero covariance but not the converse.

If $X$ and $Y$ are independent then $p(x,y) = p(x)p(y)$, so the double integral factorises and $\mathbb E[XY] = \mathbb E[X]\,\mathbb E[Y]$, giving $\operatorname{cov}(X,Y) = 0$. The converse fails because covariance only measures linear dependence. For example, if $X$ is uniform on $[-1, 1]$ and $Y = X^2$, then $\operatorname{cov}(X,Y) = \mathbb E[X^3] - \mathbb E[X]\,\mathbb E[X^2] = 0$, yet $Y$ is completely determined by $X$. For jointly Gaussian variables, however, uncorrelated does imply independent.

要点：正方向的证明（期望分解）；反例；协方差只衡量线性关系；高斯是例外。

8 What is the curse of dimensionality in nonparametric regression?

For $s$-smooth (Hölder) functions in $d$ dimensions, the minimax rate of the mean squared error is $N^{-2s/(2s+d)}$, and no estimator can do better. As $d$ increases the exponent decreases, so the number of samples needed to reach a given accuracy grows exponentially with the dimension. Intuitively, a neighbourhood of fixed radius $h$ contains a fraction of the data that shrinks like $h^d$, so local estimates become very noisy unless the neighbourhood is enlarged, which increases the bias. Greater smoothness $s$ partially offsets this, whereas a correctly specified parametric model attains $N^{-1}$ regardless of $d$.

要点：写出速率；指数随 d 减小 → 所需样本随维度指数增长；邻域里的点越来越少的直觉；与参数模型的对比。

9 What is the Bayes predictor, and why do we use empirical risk minimisation instead?

The Bayes predictor minimises the risk, the expected loss $\mathcal R(f) = \mathbb E[\ell(Y, f(X))]$ under the joint law $P(X,Y)$. For the square loss it is the conditional mean $f^\star(x) = \mathbb E[Y\mid X = x]$, because $\mathbb E[(Y - f(x))^2\mid x] = \operatorname{var}(Y\mid x) + (\mathbb E[Y\mid x] - f(x))^2$. In practice $P(X,Y)$ is unknown, so we minimise the empirical risk $\frac1N\sum_n\ell(y_n, f(x_n))$, a Monte Carlo estimate of the risk, over a restricted hypothesis class such as $f_\beta(x) = \beta^\top\phi(x)$. Without this restriction, any function interpolating the data would achieve zero empirical risk but would not generalise.

要点：风险的定义；平方损失下是条件均值（最好写出分解）；分布未知 → 经验风险是蒙特卡洛估计；必须限制假设空间。

10 Explain why LASSO can produce sparse solutions while ridge regression cannot.

Both add a norm penalty to the square error; equivalently they minimise the square error subject to $\|\beta\|_2^2\le\eta$ (ridge) or $\|\beta\|_1\le\eta$ (LASSO). The contours of the square error are ellipses centred at the least-squares solution. The $\ell_2$ ball is round, so the contours generally touch it at a point where no coefficient is zero. The $\ell_1$ ball is a diamond with corners on the coordinate axes, so the first contact often occurs at a corner, where some coefficients are exactly zero. For an orthonormal design this is explicit: ridge gives $\beta_i = b_i/(1+\lambda)$, a proportional shrinkage, whereas LASSO gives the soft threshold $\beta_i = \operatorname{sign}(b_i)\max(|b_i| - \lambda, 0)$, which sets small coefficients exactly to zero.

要点：约束形式；椭圆等高线 + 圆 vs 菱形尖角；正交设计下的两个显式公式。

11 Describe Bayesian linear regression and its relation to ridge regression.

Bayesian linear regression treats the weights as random variables with a prior $\mathcal N(0,\alpha^{-1}I)$. Combined with a Gaussian likelihood of noise precision $\eta$, the posterior is Gaussian, $\mathcal N(m, S)$ with $S = (\alpha I + \eta\Phi^\top\Phi)^{-1}$ and $m = \eta S\Phi^\top y$. Predictions average over the posterior, giving the predictive variance $\eta^{-1} + \phi(x)^\top S\phi(x)$, which quantifies uncertainty and grows away from the data. The MAP estimate is identical to ridge regression with $\lambda = \alpha/\eta$, so the ridge penalty corresponds to a Gaussian prior on the weights. The hyperparameters can be chosen by maximising the evidence (Type-II maximum likelihood) using only the training data.

要点：先验 → 后验公式；预测分布的两部分方差；MAP = 岭回归且 λ = α/η；证据最大化。

12 Explain why the soft-margin SVM can be viewed as empirical risk minimisation, and what the parameter $C$ controls.

The soft-margin SVM minimises $\frac12\|w\|^2 + C\sum_i\xi_i$ subject to $y_i f(x_i)\ge1-\xi_i$ and $\xi_i\ge0$, where $f(x) = w^\top x + b$. The constraints imply $\xi_i\ge\max(0, 1 - y_if(x_i))$, and since the objective increases with $\xi_i$, at the optimum $\xi_i = \max(0, 1 - y_if(x_i))$. Substituting gives the unconstrained problem $\min_{w,b}\frac12\|w\|^2 + C\sum_i\max(0, 1 - y_if(x_i))$, which is ERM with the (unnormalised) hinge loss plus an $\ell_2$ norm penalty, the same template as ridge regression. $C$ trades off the margin against violations: a large $C$ penalises violations heavily (narrow margin, close to the hard-margin SVM, weak regularisation), while a small $C$ allows more violations in exchange for a wider margin (strong regularisation).

要点：写出原问题；松弛变量取最小可行值；代回得到 hinge 损失 + 范数惩罚；C 的作用（≈ 1/λ）。

Formula sheet

公式速查

复习时用。考试只能带课件，这一页不能带进考场，所以要把它记熟。

概率 · Probability

加法 / 条件 / 贝叶斯$\mathbb P(A\cup B) = \mathbb P(A)+\mathbb P(B)-\mathbb P(A\cap B)$，$\mathbb P(A\mid B) = \frac{\mathbb P(A\cap B)}{\mathbb P(B)} = \frac{\mathbb P(B\mid A)\mathbb P(A)}{\mathbb P(B)}$
独立$\mathbb P(A\cap B) = \mathbb P(A)\mathbb P(B)$；$p(x,y) = p(x)p(y)$
边缘 / 链式$p(x) = \sum_y p(x,y)$ 或 $\int p(x,y)\,dy$；$p(x,y,z) = p(x\mid y,z)\,p(y\mid z)\,p(z)$
期望$\mathbb E[f(X)] = \sum_x f(x)p(x)$ 或 $\int f(x)p(x)\,dx$；$\mathbb E[aX+b] = a\mathbb E[X]+b$
方差$\operatorname{var}(X) = \mathbb E[X^2]-\mathbb E[X]^2$；$\operatorname{var}(aX+b) = a^2\operatorname{var}(X)$
协方差$\operatorname{cov}(X,Y) = \mathbb E[XY]-\mathbb E[X]\mathbb E[Y]$；$\operatorname{var}(X+Y) = \operatorname{var}X+\operatorname{var}Y+2\operatorname{cov}(X,Y)$
协方差矩阵$\operatorname{cov}(\mathbf X) = \mathbb E[\mathbf X\mathbf X^\top]-\mathbb E[\mathbf X]\mathbb E[\mathbf X]^\top$；$\operatorname{cov}(A\mathbf X+\mathbf b) = A\operatorname{cov}(\mathbf X)A^\top$

分布 · Distributions

分布p.m.f. / p.d.f.均值方差
$\operatorname{Bern}(\mu)$$\mu^x(1-\mu)^{1-x}$$\mu$$\mu(1-\mu)$
$U(a,b)$$\frac{1}{b-a}$ on $[a,b]$$\frac{a+b}{2}$$\frac{(b-a)^2}{12}$
$\mathcal N(\mu,\sigma^2)$$\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\sigma^2}}e^{-(x-\mu)^2/(2\sigma^2)}$$\mu$$\sigma^2$（$\pm1,2,3\sigma$：68.3 / 95.4 / 99.7%）
$\mathcal N(\boldsymbol\mu,\boldsymbol\Sigma)$$\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{n/2}|\boldsymbol\Sigma|^{1/2}}e^{-\frac12(\mathbf x-\boldsymbol\mu)^\top\boldsymbol\Sigma^{-1}(\mathbf x-\boldsymbol\mu)}$$\boldsymbol\mu$$\boldsymbol\Sigma$

参数回归 · Parametric regression

Bayes 预测器$f^\star = \operatorname{argmin}_f\mathbb E[\ell(Y,f(X))]$；平方损失下 $f^\star(x) = \mathbb E[Y\mid X=x]$
MLE（高斯噪声）$\beta_{\text{ML}} = (\Phi^\top\Phi)^{-1}\Phi^\top y$；$1/\eta_{\text{ML}} = \frac1N\|y - \Phi\beta_{\text{ML}}\|^2$
岭回归 / LASSO$(\Phi^\top\Phi+\lambda I)^{-1}\Phi^\top y$；正交设计：$\frac{b_i}{1+\lambda}$ vs $\operatorname{sign}(b_i)\max(|b_i|-\lambda,0)$
帽子矩阵 / LOOCV$\hat y = Hy$，$H = \Phi(\Phi^\top\Phi+\lambda I)^{-1}\Phi^\top$；$\text{LOOCV} = \frac1N\sum_n\big(\frac{y_n-\hat y_n}{1-H_{nn}}\big)^2$
GCV / 有效自由度$\text{GCV} = \frac{\frac1N\sum(y_n-\hat y_n)^2}{(1-\operatorname{tr}H/N)^2}$；$\operatorname{tr}H = \sum_j\frac{d_j^2}{d_j^2+\lambda}$（OLS 为 $p$）
局部线性 / NW$\hat\beta = (X^\top WX)^{-1}X^\top Wy$，$\hat f(x_*) = \hat\beta_0$；$\hat f_{\text{NW}}(x_*) = \frac{\sum k_h(x_n-x_*)y_n}{\sum k_h(x_n-x_*)}$
贝叶斯后验$S = (\alpha I+\eta\Phi^\top\Phi)^{-1}$，$m = \eta S\Phi^\top y$；MAP = 岭回归，$\lambda = \alpha/\eta$
预测分布$\mathcal N\big(m^\top\phi(x_*),\ \eta^{-1}+\phi(x_*)^\top S\phi(x_*)\big)$
证据最大化$\gamma = \sum_i\frac{\eta\lambda_i}{\alpha+\eta\lambda_i}$，$\alpha\leftarrow\frac{\gamma}{m^\top m}$，$\eta\leftarrow\frac{N-\gamma}{\|y-\Phi m\|^2}$

回归与核 · Regression and kernels

最小二乘$\theta = (X^\top X)^{-1}X^\top y$；一维：$a = S_{xy}/S_{xx}$，$b = \bar y - a\bar x$
岭回归$\beta = (\Phi^\top\Phi+\lambda I)^{-1}\Phi^\top y$
核岭回归$f(x) = k(x)^\top(K+\lambda I)^{-1}y$，$K_{ij} = k(x_i,x_j)$，$k(x)_i = k(x,x_i)$
合法核对称，且 $\sum_{i,j}v_iv_jk(x_i,x_j)\ge0$（Gram 矩阵半正定）
高斯 / Matérn 1/2$\theta_0e^{-\|x-x'\|^2/\theta_1}$；$\sigma^2e^{-r/\ell}$
再生性质$f(x) = \langle k(x,\cdot),f\rangle_{\mathcal H}$，$k(x,x') = \langle k(x,\cdot),k(x',\cdot)\rangle_{\mathcal H}$
表示定理$\hat f = \sum_{i=1}^n\alpha_ik(x_i,\cdot)$；$\|\hat f\|^2_{\mathcal H} = \alpha^\top K\alpha$
偏差–方差$\mathbb E[(\hat f-f)^2] = (\mathbb E\hat f-f)^2+\mathbb E[(\hat f-\mathbb E\hat f)^2]$
极小极大速率$N^{-2s/(2s+d)}$（非参数）；$N^{-1}$（设定正确的参数模型）

神经网络 · Neural networks

前向$a = Wx+b$，$z = \sigma(a)$，$\hat y = v^\top z+c$，$\ell = \frac12(\hat y-y)^2$
误差$\delta^{(o)} = \hat y-y$，$\delta^{(h)} = (\delta^{(o)}v)\odot\sigma'(a)$；深层：$\delta^{(l)} = (W_{l+1}^\top\delta^{(l+1)})\odot\sigma'(a_l)$
梯度$\frac{\partial\ell}{\partial v} = \delta^{(o)}z$，$\frac{\partial\ell}{\partial c} = \delta^{(o)}$，$\frac{\partial\ell}{\partial W} = \delta^{(h)}x^\top$，$\frac{\partial\ell}{\partial b} = \delta^{(h)}$
激活函数导数sigmoid：$z(1-z)$；tanh：$1-z^2$；ReLU：$\mathbf 1[a \gt 0]$
SGD$\theta\leftarrow\theta-\frac{\eta}{|B|}\sum_{i\in B}\nabla_\theta\mathcal L_i$；$\mathbb E[\nabla\mathcal R_B] = \nabla\mathcal R$；每 epoch 更新 $\lceil n/|B|\rceil$ 次

分类 · Classification

逻辑回归$p_\theta(x) = \sigma(\theta^\top x)$；logit $g(p) = \log\frac{p}{1-p}$；$\sigma' = \sigma(1-\sigma)$
二元交叉熵$-[y\log p + (1-y)\log(1-p)]$；NLL $= -\sum_i[y_i\theta^\top x_i - \log(1+e^{\theta^\top x_i})]$
梯度 / Hessian$X^\top(z - y)$；$X^\top RX$，$R = \operatorname{diag}(z_i(1-z_i))$，$z_i = \sigma(\theta^\top x_i)$
Newton / IRLS$\theta\leftarrow\theta - (X^\top RX)^{-1}X^\top(z - y)$
softmax$\frac{e^{\theta_k^\top x}}{\sum_je^{\theta_j^\top x}}$；多类交叉熵 $-\sum_i[\theta_{y_i}^\top x_i - \log\sum_je^{\theta_j^\top x_i}]$；$\theta_K = 0$
间隔与损失$\gamma = yf(x)$；几何间隔 $\frac{y(w^\top x+b)}{\|w\|}$；hinge $\max(0,1-\gamma)$，logistic $\log(1+e^{-\gamma})$，指数 $e^{-\gamma}$
硬 / 软间隔 SVM$\min\frac12\|w\|^2$ s.t. $y_i(w^\top x_i+b)\ge1$；$\min\frac12\|w\|^2 + C\sum_i\max(0, 1-y_if(x_i))$；间隔宽度 $2/\|w\|$
SVM 对偶$\max_\alpha\sum\alpha_i - \frac12\sum_{i,j}\alpha_i\alpha_jy_iy_jk(x_i,x_j)$，$0\le\alpha_i\le C$，$\sum\alpha_iy_i = 0$；$w = \sum\alpha_iy_ix_i$
KKT / 预测$\alpha_i(y_if(x_i)-1) = 0$；$f(x) = \sum_i\alpha_iy_ik(x_i,x) + b$，$b = y_s - w^\top x_s$
PCA（真题）保留比例 $\sum_{i\le k}\lambda_i/\sum_i\lambda_i$；$\lambda_i$ = 主方向上的方差

capacity / consistency容量 / 相合性实际表达能力 / $N\to\infty$ 时收敛到真值
LASSO / sparsity / elastic netLASSO / 稀疏性 / 弹性网L1 惩罚 / 系数恰好为 0 / L1 + L2
cross-validation / LOOCV / GCV交叉验证 / 留一 / 广义交叉验证重抽样估计泛化误差
hat matrix / leverage帽子矩阵 / 杠杆值$\hat y = Hy$ / $H_{nn}$
effective degrees of freedom有效自由度$\operatorname{tr}(H)$，有效参数个数
local polynomial / Nadaraya–Watson局部多项式 / 核回归在 $x_*$ 附近加权拟合 / 局部加权平均
bandwidth / boundary bias / LOESS带宽 / 边界偏差 / 局部加权散点平滑$h$ / 边界处一侧取平均造成的偏差 / 局部线性 + 三次立方核
prior / posterior / predictive distribution先验 / 后验 / 预测分布$p(\beta)$ / $p(\beta\mid y)$ / $p(y_*\mid x_*)$
MAP / point estimate最大后验 / 点估计后验的众数 / 只取一组参数值
evidence / marginal likelihood / Type-II ML证据 / 边缘似然 / 第二类最大似然$p(y\mid\alpha,\eta)$，对 $\beta$ 积分后最大化
model misspecification模型设定错误真实函数不在假设空间里
hypothesis space / class假设空间 / 假设类模型能表示的所有函数
heteroscedasticity异方差噪声大小随输入变化
linear smoother线性平滑器预测值对 $y$ 线性
bias–variance tradeoff偏差–方差权衡简单模型偏差大，复杂模型方差大
overfitting / underfitting过拟合 / 欠拟合追噪声 / 太简单
minimax rate / worst-case risk极小极大速率 / 最坏情况风险最好的估计量在最坏情况下的收敛速度
curse of dimensionality维数灾难维度越高收敛越慢
empirical risk minimisation (ERM)经验风险最小化最小化训练集上的平均损失
ridge regression / penalised ERM岭回归 / 惩罚 ERM加 $\frac\lambda2\|\beta\|^2$ 惩罚
primal / dual problem原问题 / 对偶问题对 $\beta$ 求解 / 对 $\alpha$ 求解
Lagrangian / Lagrange multiplier拉格朗日函数 / 拉格朗日乘子把约束并入目标
Gram (kernel) matrixGram 矩阵 / 核矩阵$K_{ij} = k(x_i,x_j)$
kernel substitution / feature map核替换 / 特征映射把内积换成核 / $\psi:\mathcal X\to\mathcal H$
lengthscale / bandwidth长度尺度 / 带宽多远的点还算相似
stationary / isotropic kernel平稳 / 各向同性核只依赖 $x-x'$ / 只依赖 $\|x-x'\|$
Hilbert space / functional / dual space希尔伯特空间 / 泛函 / 对偶空间完备内积空间 / 向量到数的映射 / 线性泛函的空间
evaluation functional求值泛函$\delta_x(f) = f(x)$
reproducing kernel / property再生核 / 再生性质$f(x) = \langle k(x,\cdot),f\rangle_{\mathcal H}$
kernel section核截面$k(x,\cdot)$
representer theorem表示定理最优解 $= \sum_i\alpha_ik(x_i,\cdot)$
layer / weight / bias / activation层 / 权重 / 偏置 / 激活$a = Wx+b$，$z = \sigma(a)$
multilayer perceptron (MLP)多层感知机前馈、全连接的网络
feedforward / fully connected (dense)前馈 / 全连接只向前传 / 层间两两相连
architecture架构层数、宽度、激活函数等
backpropagation / forward pass反向传播 / 前向传播逐层往回用链式法则 / 从输入算到输出
Jacobian / Hadamard product雅可比矩阵 / 逐元素积$\partial z_i/\partial a_j$ / $\odot$
stochastic gradient descent / mini-batch / epoch随机梯度下降 / 小批量 / 轮用部分数据估计梯度 / 每次用的子集 / 完整遍历一次
learning rate学习率每步走多远 $\eta$
unbiased无偏期望等于真值
discriminant function / decision boundary判别函数 / 决策边界$\hat y = \operatorname{sign}(g(x))$ / $g(x) = 0$
hyperplane超平面$w^\top x + b = 0$，$p-1$ 维
logistic regression / sigmoid逻辑回归 / S 型函数$p = \sigma(w^\top x + b)$
odds / log-odds (logit)几率 / 对数几率$\frac{p}{1-p}$ / $\log\frac{p}{1-p}$
generalised linear model / link function广义线性模型 / 链接函数$g(\mathbb E[Y\mid x]) = w^\top x + b$
cross-entropy / log loss交叉熵 / 对数损失伯努利或类别分布的负对数似然
naive Bayes / likelihood ratio朴素贝叶斯 / 似然比特征条件独立的生成式模型 / $p(x\mid s)/p(x\mid f)$
softmax / one-hot / simplexsoftmax / 独热编码 / 单纯形多类概率 / 标签向量 / 概率向量所在的集合
reference class / identifiability参考类 / 可辨识性只需 $K-1$ 个概率 / 参数唯一（$\theta_K = 0$）
Newton–Raphson / Hessian / IRLS牛顿法 / 海森矩阵 / 迭代重加权最小二乘$\theta - H^{-1}\nabla f$ / 二阶导矩阵 / 逻辑回归的 Newton 法
surrogate loss / margin替代损失 / 间隔0–1 损失的代用品 / $\gamma = yf(x)$
geometric margin / hinge loss几何间隔 / 合页损失到边界的有符号距离 / $\max(0, 1-\gamma)$
support vector machine / support vectors支持向量机 / 支持向量最大间隔分类器 / $\alpha_i\gt0$ 的点
slack variables / soft margin松弛变量 / 软间隔允许违反间隔的 $\xi_i$
KKT conditions / complementary slacknessKKT 条件 / 互补松弛$\alpha_ig_i = 0$
kernel SVM核支持向量机$f(x) = \sum_i\alpha_iy_ik(x_i,x) + b$
PCA / principal direction主成分分析 / 主方向协方差矩阵的特征向量；特征值 = 方差
Errata

课件勘误汇总

开卷考试只能带课件，建议把这些位置标出来。页码是 PDF 的页码。试卷参考答案里的问题写在真题精讲各题的“注意”框里。

位置课件写法正确写法
L1 p.12骰子点数 $V = \{0,1,\dots,6\}$$V = \{1,\dots,6\}$（小笔误）
L1 p.14Sum of probability of independent eventsmutually exclusive events（互斥事件）
L1 p.28$\mathbb E[X] = \frac{b-a}{2}$$\mathbb E[X] = \frac{a+b}{2}$
L1 p.33$p(x_1,\dots,x_n) = p(x_1\mid x_2)\,p(x_2\mid x_3)\cdots p(x_n)$$p(x_1\mid x_2,\dots,x_n)\,p(x_2\mid x_3,\dots,x_n)\cdots p(x_n)$；课件写法只在马尔可夫假设下成立
L1 p.43二次型 $\gt 0$$\ge 0$（在 $\mathbf x = \boldsymbol\mu$ 处等于 0）
L2 pp.6–7$\beta_0+\dots+\beta_px^{p-1}$，$\beta\in\mathbb R^p$$\beta_px^p$，$\beta\in\mathbb R^{p+1}$
L2 p.25方框标题 “ML estimate of β”“ML estimate of η”（框里是噪声精度）
L2 p.48ln λ = 0.1，ln λ = 10⁶λ = 0.1，λ = 10⁶
L2 p.51LOOCV：“set k = 1, then we have N folds”k = N
L2 p.63$U\in\mathbb R^{N\times p}$ (if $N\lt p$)，$U^\top U = UU^\top = I$$N\gt p$；瘦 SVD 只有 $U^\top U = I$
L2 p.74$\beta_i$ 是 $i$ 阶导数$\beta_i = f^{(i)}(x_*)/i!$
L2 p.86“tri-weight kernel” $(1-u^3)^3$这个公式是 tricube（三次立方）核
L2 p.87$\beta_i$ independent with precision $\alpha^{-1}$精度 $\alpha$（方差 $\alpha^{-1}$）
L2 p.101$\gamma = 1 - \sum_i\frac{1}{\alpha+\eta\lambda_i}$$\gamma = p - \sum_i\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\eta\lambda_i} = \sum_i\frac{\eta\lambda_i}{\alpha+\eta\lambda_i}$
L2 其他p.26、28 的 θ；p.43 的 ‖θ‖₁；p.50 循环里的 “GS-CV”；p.58 的 Xᵀy；p.102 的 Xmβ；‖β‖₁；k-fold CV；Φᵀy；Φm（记号笔误）
L3 p.22$\alpha = (\lambda I+K)^{-1}y$$\alpha = \lambda(K+\lambda I)^{-1}y$（最终的 KRR 公式不受影响）
L3 p.23$\beta = -\frac1\lambda\Phi^\top\alpha$$\beta = +\frac1\lambda\Phi^\top\alpha$（与 p.20 一致）
L3 p.24核必须非负 $k(x,x')\ge0$不需要；只需对称 + 半正定（线性核可以为负）
L3 p.67$\delta^{(h)} = (\delta^{(o)}v)\odot\sigma'(z)$$\sigma'(a)$
L3 p.72$\frac1n\sum_{i\in B}\nabla\mathcal L_i = \nabla\mathcal R$$\frac1n\sum_{i=1}^n\nabla\mathcal L_i = \nabla\mathcal R$
L4 p.19$\mathcal R(\theta) = \frac1n\mathcal L(\theta)$（$\mathcal L$ 是对数似然）$\mathcal R(\theta) = -\frac1n\mathcal L(\theta)$
L4 p.29$\theta_k^\top = 0$；$(\theta_i - \theta_i)^\top x = 0$$\theta_k^\top x = 0$；$(\theta_i - \theta_j)^\top x = 0$
L4 p.32$\theta_{y_i}^\top x_i = \log\sum_je^{\theta_j^\top x_i}$$\theta_{y_i}^\top x_i - \log\sum_je^{\theta_j^\top x_i}$
L4 p.33Newton 法 “return to step 3”回到第 2 步（重新计算梯度和 Hessian）
L4 p.40$|d| = y(w^\top x+b)/\|w\| = \gamma$只对分对的点成立；一般地 $\gamma$ 是有符号距离
L4 p.55$\alpha_i = 0$ (non-support vector, active constraint)inactive constraint（$y_if(x_i)\gt1$）；后半句缺 “$= 0$”

另外两处不算笔误、但值得注意的说法：L1 p.21 用 “most likely value” 引入均值，但均值一般不是最可能的值（那是众数）；L1 p.26 把伯努利分布的参数 μ 称为 hyperparameter，多数教材称之为 parameter。
